Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.

</details>


### [145] [EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13662)
*Amy Xin,Jiening Siow,Junjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Fanjin Zhang,Jian Song,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出环境工程（Environment Engineering）是自主科学发现的关键瓶颈，并构建了EurekAgent系统，通过权限、工件、预算和人在环路四个维度的环境设计，在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得了新SOTA结果（如26圆填充问题，总API成本低于11美元）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体能力的提升，自主科学发现的瓶颈正从设计智能体工作流转向设计智能体环境（资源、约束和接口）。现有方法缺乏对环境的系统性工程化设计，导致探索行为受限、奖励破解及人工监督摩擦大等问题。

Method: 提出EurekAgent系统，围绕四个维度进行环境工程：(1) 权限工程——限制智能体执行范围并隔离评估环境；(2) 工件工程——基于文件系统和Git的协作管理；(3) 预算工程——预算感知的探索机制；(4) 人在环路工程——简化人工监督与干预。

Result: 在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得SOTA结果，其中26圆填充问题以不到11美元API成本实现新SOTA。代码和结果已开源。

Conclusion: 环境工程应成为开发可靠自主研究智能体的核心研究方向，系统性地设计环境可有效放大有益行为、抑制有害行为。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.

</details>


### [146] [Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13591)
*Ali Elahi,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三种协议，将多智能体辩论（MAD）中各模型的置信度信号转化为可比较的数值，并通过软投票或贝叶斯融合聚合为系统统一的置信度。该聚合置信度在判别力（AUARC）上显著优于最佳单智能体或标准辩论基线，同时保持F1分数稳定，并弥补了MAD在模糊任务上的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP置信度评估方法仅适用于单模型，多智能体辩论虽利用置信度加权消息或触发辩论，但未将各智能体置信度聚合为系统整体的单一置信度，缺乏对多智能体系统输出可靠性进行统一度量的方法。

Method: 设计三种聚合协议：(1) 将原始置信度信号通过校准器（参数/非参数）转化使其跨模型可比；(2) 通过软投票或概率融合（贝叶斯融合）聚合为单一置信度。分析了序列概率和自我报告两种估计器，以及参数和非参数校准器的影响。

Result: 聚合置信度在AUARC上显著优于最佳单智能体和标准辩论基线；F1分数稳定且恢复了MAD在模糊任务上的损失；校准对F1有提升但对AUARC影响较小；在5个基准、4种任务类型、6组同构/异构辩论对上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的聚合置信度协议能有效为多智能体系统提供可靠的统一置信度，兼具强判别力与稳定性，为多智能体系统的可靠性评估和下游决策提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [147] [EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13602)
*Harihara Muralidharan,Reema Baskar,Soo Hee Lee,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiBench是一个用于短周期表观基因组学分析的可验证基准测试平台，包含106个评估任务，覆盖CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化等流程。在16个模型-工具组合的5,088条有效轨迹中，最佳系统GPT-5.5/Pi通过率仅45.0%，多数系统表现不佳，失败原因在于缺乏深度的实验特异性科学判断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试难以评估AI在表观基因组学分析中做出合理分析决策的能力，缺乏一个可验证的、确定性评分的基准来测试智能体是否能在真实的流程状态下做出正确分析判断。

Method: 构建EpiBench基准，包含来自CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化流程的106个评估任务。通过16个模型-工具组合生成5,088条有效轨迹，采用确定性评分标准进行可验证评估。

Result: 最佳系统GPT-5.5/Pi通过率45.0%（143/318次尝试；95% CI 36.3-53.7），GPT-5.5/OpenAI Codex通过率39.9%（127/318；95% CI 31.6-48.3），Claude Opus 4.8 Max/Pi和GPT-5.4/Pi各通过39.0%（124/318；95% CI 30.2-47.8和31.0-47.0）。没有系统通过大多数尝试，且表现因检测类型而异。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在表观基因组学分析中能定位文件并计算中间结果，但在需要深度实验特异性科学判断的任务上普遍失败，表明在领域专业知识推理方面仍需改进。

Abstract: We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.

</details>


### [148] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13604)
*Haochen Wu,Yi Hou,Shiguang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DoorDash在大型食品配送市场中部署的强化学习系统，该系统利用延迟的世界反馈信号来调整调度目标权重。通过一个存储级策略选择离散乘数，改变配送质量和批处理效率之间的权衡，从而在保持生产可行性和操作安全约束的前提下实现离线策略学习。实验表明，该离线训练策略在提高批处理效率、降低配送时间成本的同时，不影响客户交付质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三边市场中的调度问题为基于世界反馈的强化学习提供了自然场景，但决策受到延迟操作结果（如配送速度、骑手利用率和商家拥堵）的评估。需要一种能够安全适应实时经济和物流系统反馈的决策策略调整方法。

Method: 提出一个部署的强化学习系统：1) 不替换组合分配优化器，而是通过存储级策略从离线市场数据中学习，选择离散乘数来调整调度优化器在配送质量与批处理效率之间的权衡。2) 使用集中式离线数据和分散式存储级执行训练共享价值函数，采用双Q学习目标及保守正则化器减少分布外价值高估。

Result: 在生产环境的分流实验中，离线训练的策略增加了批处理效率，降低了骑手侧时间成本，且未降低面向客户的配送质量。

Conclusion: 展示了如何利用实时经济和物流系统中的世界反馈，安全地在线调整决策策略，验证了在噪声、延迟和耦合反馈条件下进行离线策略学习的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

</details>


### [149] [Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13607)
*Zach Studdiford,Gary Lupyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 人类与LLM在日常因果推理中均表现出类似的模式匹配错误，而非基于抽象世界模型的理性推理；通过分析LLM的注意力头可预测人类的推理错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳"LLM的推理失败（如泛化不足、随机错误）证明其并非真正推理而只是模式匹配"这一观点，通过对比人类与LLM的推理错误模式，质疑人类推理是否真的基于抽象世界模型。

Method: 1) 评估人类参与者与25种LLM在多种日常情境下的常识推理能力；2) 识别驱动LLM响应的注意力头集合，分析其实现模式匹配的机制；3) 利用这些注意力头预测人类因看似无关的提示细节而产生的推理错误。

Result: 人类与LLM在常识推理中表现出相似类型的错误模式；LLM的注意力头实现了模式匹配机制；这些注意力头可以预测人类因无关提示细节导致的看似不合理的推理错误。

Conclusion: 人类与LLM的日常因果推理更符合模式匹配机制，而非基于抽象世界模型的理论推理。

Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.

</details>


### [150] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA)评估框架，通过标准化协议(A2A和MCP)实现智能体无关的统一评估，并基于AgentBeats系统在五个月开放竞赛和编码案例研究中验证其覆盖度、实用性和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估碎片化，大多数基准依赖于固定的大模型评估框架，存在集成负担重、测试与生产环境不匹配、难以公平比较不同智能体设计等问题，根本原因是缺乏开放且与智能体无关的评估接口。

Method: 提出AAA框架，使用评判智能体(Judge Agent)执行评估，所有参与者通过标准化协议交互：A2A(任务管理)和MCP(工具访问)。该框架将评估逻辑与智能体实现解耦，仅需单一接口。进一步实现AgentBeats系统，识别五种实际运行模式以适应开放性、隐私性和可复现性约束。

Result: 五个月开放竞赛中收集298个评判智能体(12个类别)和467个被评估智能体，验证AAA在异构基准上的适用性；编码智能体案例研究证实该方法保持与公开记录一致的保真度，同时揭示了此前缺失的横向对比结果。

Conclusion: AAA和AgentBeats为开放、标准化、可复现的智能体评估提供了清晰路径，通过社区规模现场研究和受控编码案例研究验证了其在异构场景下的覆盖度、实用性和保真度。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [151] [Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13658)
*Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini,Massimo Chiriatti,Enrico Panai,Giuseppe Riva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种理解AI认知与 epistemic 后果的框架（三系统理论、Thinkframes和系统0），提出系统0具有独特理论地位，并引入"认知殖民"概念，指出AI系统能将外部利益嵌入自我架构中，产生难以察觉的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三种框架（三系统理论、Thinkframes、系统0）对AI认知与知识论后果的解释力尚未系统比较，且缺乏对AI隐性影响机制的深入理论刻画。

Method: 通过理论比较分析，论证系统0的独特地位，并引入"认知殖民"这一新概念来补充现有框架的解释力。

Result: 三系统理论和Thinkframes捕捉了AI对个体推理和集体认知实践的重要维度，但系统0占据了一个两者都无法完全复制的独特理论位置；"认知殖民"描述了AI将外部利益嵌入自我认知架构的机制。

Conclusion: 理解AI系统隐形影响形式是紧迫的哲学与实践任务，因为这类系统已广泛部署。

Abstract: This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.

</details>


### [152] [Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669)
*Zongsheng Cao,Bihao Zhan,Jinxin Shi,Jiong Wang,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Zijie Guo,Tianshuo Peng,Zhuo Liu,Yi Xie,Xiang Zhuang,Yue Fan,Runmin Ma,Shiyang Feng,Xiangchao Yan,Anran Liu,Peng Ye,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agents-K1，一个端到端的知识编排管线，将科学文献转化为智能体原生知识图谱，并构建了包含246万篇论文的Scholar-KG知识图谱，在科学信息抽取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的研究智能体侧重于智能体编排，忽视了科学知识编排。现有工作通常将论文简化为摘要、表面提及和扁平引用边，缺少科学推理所需的关键实体、声明、证据、机制和方法谱系等核心要素。

Method: Agents-K1包含三个组件：(1)多模态解析器，五模块模式覆盖实体、多模态证据、引用及跨实体关系；(2)基于GRPO训练的4B信息抽取骨干网络；(3)graph-anything CLI，统一网络搜索、多模态图谱检索和跨文档遍历的三源智能体接口。在此基础上处理了246万篇科学论文构建Scholar-KG。

Result: 在科学信息抽取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理任务上取得优异性能；发布了包含100万篇论文的子集，完整Scholar-KG可通过SCP链接访问；该管线可扩展到通用领域语料和符合模式的数据合成。

Conclusion: Agents-K1通过系统化的知识编排弥补了现有研究智能体在科学知识表示上的不足，证明了深度知识图谱构建对科学推理的提升作用，且具有跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [153] [Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13670)
*Tobias Holtdirk,Pietro Marcolongo,Anna Steinberg Schulten,Felix Henninger,Stefan Rose,Sarah Ball,Bolei Ma,Frauke Kreuter,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明大型语言模型（LLMs）可以自动化社会科学和行为科学中研究的可重复性评估。使用76项已发表研究，LLM管道在41%的研究中恢复了原始效应量（Cohen's d ±0.05容忍度），在96%的情况下得出了与原始研究相同的定性结论，优于人类再分析者（34%效应量恢复，74%定性结论一致）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可重复性评估依赖独立研究者重新分析原始数据，这种方法资源密集且难以规模化。作者希望利用LLMs自动化这一过程，以解决可扩展性问题。

Method: 使用N=76项已发表的行为与社会科学研究（带有预定义声明），将LLM生成的分析与原始发现及人类再分析进行比较。LLM管道尝试从原始论文中提取信息并生成效应量估计，与原始结果和人类再分析结果进行对比。

Result: 7项研究LLM无法生成可行的效应量估计；剩余研究中，LLM在41%的研究中恢复了原始效应量（±0.05 Cohen's d容忍度），在96%的情况下得出与原始研究相同的定性结论。相比之下，人类再分析者在34%的研究中恢复原始效应量，在74%的情况下得出相同的定性结论。

Conclusion: LLMs可以作为可扩展的工具用于自动化可重复性评估，为社会科学和行为科学中实证结果的系统性审计提供基础。

Abstract: Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Influence Factors on RAG Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12469)
*Pedro Pereira,Eva Maia,Isabel Praça,Adrien Bécue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过432种配置的全因子实验研究，系统分析了检索增强生成（RAG）系统在面对投毒攻击时的鲁棒性。研究发现，检索器架构、数据集和检索深度是影响投毒暴露的最强因素，而生成器选择和数据库组成则对下游攻击成功率有重大影响。密集检索器和图检索器相比BM25更鲁棒，但更大的检索深度会增加检索到投毒内容的概率。投毒内容在多个数据库中的复制会放大对抗性影响，而额外的干净来源可以缓解这种影响。投毒脆弱性源于检索、生成和知识库配置的交互作用，而非单一组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖外部检索文档来增强大语言模型，但这种对外部检索内容的依赖引入了投毒攻击的漏洞——对抗性文档可以操纵检索过程和生成输出。现有研究缺乏对RAG系统投毒鲁棒性的系统性理解，本文旨在通过大规模全因子实验填补这一空白。

Method: 采用全因子实验设计，覆盖432种不同配置，系统性地操纵数据集、检索器类型、检索深度、数据库组成、分块策略和生成器模型六个因素，在检索级别和生成级别两个维度评估投毒攻击的影响。

Result: 1) 检索器架构、数据集和检索深度是影响投毒暴露的最强因素；2) 生成器选择和数据库组成对下游攻击成功率影响最大；3) 密集检索器和图检索器比BM25更鲁棒；4) 更大检索深度增加投毒内容被检索到的概率；5) 跨数据库复制投毒内容放大对抗影响，而额外干净来源可缓解。

Conclusion: RAG系统的投毒脆弱性并非源于单一组件，而是检索、生成和知识库配置三者交互作用的结果。这提示防御策略需要系统性考虑整个RAG管道的协同优化。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems enhance large language models by grounding responses in retrieved documents from external knowledge sources at inference time. However, this reliance on retrieved content introduces vulnerabilities to poisoning attacks, in which adversarial documents can manipulate both the retrieval process and the generated outputs. This paper investigates poisoning robustness in RAG through a full factorial experimental study covering 432 configurations. We analyze the impacts of dataset, retriever type, retrieval depth, database composition, chunking strategy, and generator model on retrieval-level and generation-level metrics. The results show that retriever architecture, dataset, and retrieval depth are the strongest factors affecting poisoning exposure, while generator choice and database composition have a major impact on downstream attack success. Dense and graph-based retrievers generally improve robustness relative to BM25, whereas larger retrieval depth increases the likelihood of retrieving poisoned passages. We further show that replicating poisoned content across multiple databases amplifies adversarial influence, while additional clean sources can mitigate it. These findings highlight that poisoning vulnerability in RAG is not attributable to a single component, but instead arises from the interaction of retrieval, generation, and knowledge-base configuration.

</details>


### [155] [From Parameters to Feature Space: Task Arithmetic for Backdoor Mitigation in Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12498)
*Zhenqian Zhu,Yamin Hu,Yiya Diao,Weixiang Li,Haodong Li,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出线性特征路径最小化（LFPM）框架，通过在特征空间中引入反后门任务向量来消除模型合并中的后门攻击，同时保持干净任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并容易受到后门攻击，现有基于任务算术的防御方法因依赖直接参数空间编辑，在消除后门时严重损害干净任务性能。

Method: 基于跨任务线性（CTL）框架，从统一特征空间视角将后门鲁棒性形式化；引入反后门任务向量，通过梯度累积和损失路径积分优化机制沿插值路径抑制后门。

Result: 在全微调和参数高效微调（PEFT）设置下，LFPM对后门攻击均表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LFPM有效解决了模型合并中后门防御与干净任务性能保持之间的权衡问题，优于现有基于任务算术的防御方法。

Abstract: Model merging (MM) has gained significant attention as a cost-effective approach to integrate multiple task-specific models into a unified model. However, recent work reveals that MM is highly susceptible to backdoor attacks. Existing defenses based on task arithmetic often fail to eliminate backdoors without substantially degrading clean-task performance, owing to their reliance on direct parameter-space editing. To address this gap, we propose Linear Feature Path Minimization (LFPM), a backdoor mitigation framework for model merging, which introduces an anti-backdoor task vector into the backdoored merged model. Unlike prior approaches, LFPM formulates the backdoor robustness of the merged model from a unified feature-space perspective under the Cross-Task Linearity (CTL) framework, which leverages the approximate linearity of features across tasks. This perspective guides the optimization of the anti-backdoor task to suppress backdoors while preserving clean-task performance. Furthermore, we introduce an effective optimization mechanism based on gradient accumulation and loss path-integral, ensuring robust backdoor suppression along the interpolation path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LFPM consistently exhibits strong robustness against backdoor attacks in both full fine-tuning and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) settings.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Attack Success Rate: Examining Trigger Leakage in Vision-Language Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12586)
*Jiamin Chang,Salil Kanhere,Piotr Koniusz,Jason,Xue,Hammond Pearce*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉后门攻击中的"触发器泄漏"问题——与目标触发器视觉或语义相近的输入会意外激活隐藏行为，并提出了邻域泄漏率（NLR）进行量化。实验发现标准微调会学习到宽泛的激活区域，而加入编辑距离为1的难负样本可显著收紧该区域、降低泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注干净准确率和ASR，无法衡量攻击是否"精确"（即仅在预期触发器出现时才激活隐藏行为），缺乏对触发器泄漏问题的形式化定义和量化指标。

Method: 形式化定义触发器泄漏，提出邻域泄漏率（NLR）量化指标；以文本触发器为可控探针，通过添加编辑距离为1的难负样本进行训练来收紧激活区域。

Result: 3%投毒率下，图标和文本触发器对常见视觉变换保持鲁棒，但相邻变体严重泄漏（图标NLR=0.996，文本NLR=0.944）；加入难负样本后激活区域显著收窄，泄漏降低，并在图像编辑和具身操作流程中验证了效果。

Conclusion: 标准微调学习到的是宽泛激活区域而非精确触发条件，导致泄漏；通过有针对性的难负样本训练可有效提升后门攻击的精确性，避免泄漏传播到可执行程序和动作序列中。

Abstract: Vision-Language Agentic Systems (VLAS) connect visual perception to planning, tool use, and physical actions. This means backdoor-type triggers can propagate through both decision pipelines and their connected interfaces, thus making visual backdoors a system-level threat. Current evaluations on such backdoors focus on clean accuracy and attack success rate (ASR), metrics that capture whether a trigger works, but not whether an attack is actually "precise" -- i.e. whether it triggers hidden behaviors only when intended. In this work, we formalize the failure of trigger precision as "trigger leakage": inputs that are visually or semantically close to the intended trigger and therefore inadvertently activate the attacker-specified behavior. To quantify this leakage, we introduce Neighbor Leakage Rate (NLR). Our experiments show that at a 3% poisoning ratio, icon and text triggers remain robust to common visual transformations, but their neighboring variants leak heavily, with NLR reaching 0.996 (icon) and 0.944 (text). Using textual triggers as a controlled probe, we show that standard fine-tuning learns a broad activation region rather than an exact trigger condition, causing neighboring strings to invoke the malicious behavior even when the exact trigger is absent. Adding edit-distance-one hard-negative samples during training substantially narrows this activation region and reduces leakage, including in image-editing and embodied-manipulation workflows, where leaked triggers can propagate into executable programs and action sequences.

</details>


### [157] [Amnesia: A Stealthy Replay Attack on Continual Learning Dreams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12655)
*Ahmed Sharshar,Naveen Kumar Kummari,Mohsen Guizani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Amnesia攻击，通过仅控制回放索引选择（不修改像素、标签或模型参数），在可审计约束（类别直方图散度预算和回放率预算）下最大化持续学习模型的性能退化，揭示了回放控制作为实用威胁面的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习攻击要么修改输入或训练流程（投毒/后门），要么缺乏显式的可审计约束，不够现实。本文旨在探索一种更真实、受限的威胁：仅控制回放索引选择的内部攻击者，在可审计约束下能否有效退化模型性能。

Method: 提出Amnesia攻击，两步法：(i) 计算轻量级类别效用（如EMA损失或置信度），将名义类别直方图p0向有害类别倾斜；(ii) 通过KL（指数倾斜）或TV（平衡质量重分配）优化器将倾斜投影回δ-ball内。窗口调度器确保滚动审计合规。

Result: 在多个CL基准和强回放基线中，Amnesia一致降低最终准确率（ACC）并恶化后向迁移（-BWT）。KL变体高影响且难以检测；TV变体破坏性更强但更容易被检测到，尤其在严格的每类约束下。

Conclusion: 仅回放索引控制构成了持续学习系统中一种实用的、可审计的威胁面，建立了原则性的影响-可见性权衡。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) models often use experience replay to reduce catastrophic forgetting, but their robustness to replay sampling interference remains underexplored. Existing CL attacks alter inputs or training pipelines (poisoning/backdoors) and rarely include explicit auditable constraints, limiting realism. Here, auditability means a monitor can verify compliance from sampler-visible telemetry - e.g., logged replay index/label statistics - by checking that the realized replay class histogram stays close to a nominal baseline and that replay rate is unchanged per batch and/or over a rolling window. We study a limited-privilege insider who controls only replay index selection, not pixels, labels, or model parameters, while staying within auditable limits such as queue priorities. We introduce Amnesia, a replay composition attack that maximizes degradation under two budgets: a visibility budget delta bounding the TV/KL divergence from a nominal class histogram p0, and a mass budget f fixing the replay rate. Amnesia has two steps: (i) compute lightweight class utilities, such as EMA loss or confidence, to tilt p0 toward harmful classes; and (ii) project the tilt back into the delta-ball using efficient KL (exponential tilt) or TV (balanced mass redistribution) optimizers. A windowed scheduler enforces rolling audits. Across challenging CL benchmarks and strong replay baselines, Amnesia consistently lowers final accuracy (ACC) and worsens backward transfer (-BWT). The KL variant delivers high impact while remaining largely undetected under multiple audit schemes, including per-batch and rolling-window checks. The TV variant is more damaging but easier to detect, especially under tight per-class constraints. These results expose index-only replay control as a practical, auditable threat surface in CL systems and establish a principled impact-visibility trade-off.

</details>


### [158] [CAPED: Context-Aware Privacy Exposure Defense for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12666)
*Siyu Shen,Fenghao Xu,Wenrui Diao,Kehuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPED，一种面向移动GUI代理的上下文感知预上传曝光控制层。在截图发送到远程多模态代理之前，CAPED提取任务需求、利用屏幕上下文作为隐私先验、解析可见UI元素，选择性暴露任务所需内容同时遮蔽无关隐私内容。在AndroidWorld评估中，CAPED将加权种子泄露从0.766降至0.268，同时保持高任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于截图的移动GUI代理将每次屏幕观察变成隐私边界——截图可能暴露与用户请求无关的敏感上下文（联系人、消息、照片、健康提示等），即"偶然视觉隐私暴露"问题。现有文本匿名化方法遗漏视觉和推断性线索，通用隐私遮蔽可能移除GUI代理完成任务所需的证据和控制。

Method: CAPED设计为手机端保护层，在截图发布到远程多模态代理前执行：提取任务需求 → 使用屏幕上下文作为隐私先验 → 解析可见UI元素 → 选择性暴露当前任务所需内容，同时遮蔽无关的偶然隐私内容。

Result: 在28个任务的种子隐私评估中，完整CAPED将成功条件加权种子泄露从原始截图的0.766降至0.268，同时保持高任务效用。在更广泛的AndroidWorld测试中，存在原型级别的效用成本，但结果支持核心主张。

Conclusion: 截图上传应被视为明确的设备-云边界决策，由任务驱动的选择性曝光而非全有或全无的屏幕共享来管控。

Abstract: Screenshot-based mobile GUI agents can operate ordinary smartphone apps through the same visual interface as a human user, but this capability also turns every screen observation into a privacy boundary. During normal task execution, screenshots may expose contacts, messages, photos, files, recommendations, health cues, and other sensitive context that is unrelated to the user's request. We call this problem incidental visual privacy exposure. It is difficult to address with existing defenses: text anonymization misses many visual and inferential cues, while generic privacy masking can remove the evidence and controls that a GUI agent needs to complete the task.
  This paper presents CAPED, a context-aware pre-upload exposure control layer for mobile GUI agents. CAPED is designed as a phone-side protection layer: before screenshots are released to a remote multimodal agent, it extracts task requirements, uses screen context as a privacy prior, parses visible UI elements, and selectively exposes only content needed for the current task while masking incidental private content. We evaluate CAPED on AndroidWorld for broad task utility and with a controlled 28-task seeded privacy evaluation used as a measurement instrument for trajectory-level incidental leakage. In this seeded evaluation, Full CAPED reduces success-conditioned weighted seeded leakage from 0.766 under raw screenshots to 0.268 while preserving high task utility. A broader AndroidWorld run shows a remaining prototype-level utility cost, but the results support the central claim that screenshot upload should be treated as an explicit device--cloud boundary decision, governed by task-driven selective exposure rather than all-or-nothing screen sharing.

</details>


### [159] [SMSR: Certified Defence Against Runtime Memory Poisoning in Persistent LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12703)
*Tarun Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对RAG代理多会话记忆投毒攻击（MSMP）的防御框架SMSR，通过签名认证（HMAC-SHA256）和基于随机记忆消除的平滑检索投票机制，首次实现了对该场景的可认证鲁棒性保证，实验表明攻击成功率从93-100%降至0%（未签名）和8.0%（认证攻击者单次注入）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG代理在跨用户会话中累积持久记忆，产生了新的攻击面：攻击者可通过正常交互注入恶意记忆，在后续被其他用户检索时操纵代理响应，而现有防御（如静态语料库防护、启发式过滤器）均无法有效应对这种多会话记忆投毒攻击。

Method: SMSR包含两个组件：组件1在写入时添加HMAC-SHA256来源认证，阻止未签名注入；组件2在查询时采用随机记忆消除（randomised memory ablation）结合基于判断（verdict-based）的多数投票机制，限制已认证攻击者的影响力，并形式化推导了超几何证书和一致性少数效应。

Result: 在15个企业场景（3150次重复试验）中，组件1将未签名变种的攻击成功率从93-100%降至0%；对单次注入的认证攻击者，组件2将成功率控制在8.0%（95% CI [5.8, 10.9], n=450）；端到端仅查询攻击中，SMSR将成功率从65.3%降至5.3%（n=150）；干净查询效用分别为90%（组件1）和85%（组合）。

Conclusion: SMSR是首个针对多会话记忆投毒攻击提供可认证鲁棒性边界的防御方案，证明了无来源认证的检索时过滤器无法应对自适应注入，并形式化了基于字符串投票与基于判断投票的关键差异（一致性少数效应），为RAG系统的持久记忆安全提供了新的理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) agents increasingly run with persistent memory that accumulates across user sessions. This creates a new attack surface: an adversary interacting only through normal channels can inject crafted memories that, once retrieved, steer the agent's responses for future users, without touching model weights or code. We call this Multi-Session Memory Poisoning (MSMP) and show that no existing defence certifies against it; static-corpus defences (RobustRAG, ReliabilityRAG) assume a fixed knowledge base, and heuristic filters are bypassed by fluent enterprise-style text. We present Signed Memory with Smoothed Retrieval (SMSR), the first defence with a certified robustness bound for this setting. Component 1 adds HMAC-SHA256 provenance at write time, blocking unsigned injection. Component 2 applies randomised memory ablation with verdict-based majority voting at query time, bounding the influence of authenticated adversaries. We prove that no provenance-free retrieval-time filter can certify against adaptive injection, derive a hypergeometric certificate for Component 2, and formalise the Consistent Minority Effect, whereby a consistent adversarial answer wins string-based voting as a numerical minority while verdict-based voting removes it. Across 15 enterprise scenarios (3,150 repeated trials), Component 1 cuts attack success from 93-100% to 0% for all unsigned variants. For an authenticated adversary with a single injection, Component 2 holds success to 8.0% (95% CI [5.8, 10.9], n=450), below the certified worst case. In an end-to-end query-only attack where the agent itself writes the poison rather than it being pre-seeded, SMSR reduces success from 65.3% to 5.3% (n=150, non-overlapping CIs) on a live agent stack. Clean-query utility is 90% (Component 1) and 85% (combined).

</details>


### [160] [PI-Hunter: Automated Red-Teaming for Exposing and Localizing Prompt Injections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12737)
*Pengfei He,Lesly Miculicich,Vishesh Sharma,Ash Fox,George Lee,Jiliang Tang,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PI-Hunter，一种自动化智能体审计框架，用于主动暴露LLM智能体中的潜在提示注入漏洞，通过构建真实源感知测试用例并迭代演化，显著提升了漏洞暴露和攻击面覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要关注推理时阻断恶意内容，红队方法主要优化攻击成功率，导致开发者对潜在提示注入如何在智能体中产生和传播的可见性有限。

Method: PI-Hunter构建真实源感知测试用例，并通过反馈驱动的探索迭代演化，诱导智能体从外部环境中检索并暴露隐藏的恶意指令。

Result: 在多个基准测试、智能体架构、攻击和防御设置下的实验表明，PI-Hunter显著优于强自动化红队基线，提升了漏洞暴露和攻击面覆盖率，且在现有提示注入防御下仍然有效。

Conclusion: PI-Hunter为LLM智能体提供了一种主动的漏洞暴露方法，弥补了现有防御和红队方法的不足，有效揭示了间接提示注入风险。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into agentic systems that interact with external tools and environments, introducing new security risks such as indirect prompt injection attacks through untrusted external sources. Existing defenses mainly focus on blocking malicious content at inference time, and current red-teaming methods primarily optimize attack success. As a result, developers have limited visibility into how latent prompt injections emerge and propagate through agents. We propose PI-Hunter, an automated agentic auditing framework for proactive vulnerability exposure in LLM agents. PI-Hunter constructs realistic source-aware test cases and iteratively evolves them through feedback-driven exploration to induce agents to retrieve and reveal latent malicious instructions embedded within external environments. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks, agent architectures, attacks, and defenses demonstrate that PI-Hunter substantially improves vulnerability exposure and attack-surface coverage over strong automated red-teaming baselines, while remaining effective under existing prompt injection defenses.

</details>


### [161] [Semantic Identification of IoT Devices from Behavioral Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12793)
*Samuel Witt,Hassan Habibi Gharakheili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于制造商使用说明（MUD）配置文件的物联网设备识别方法，利用ACE级别语义表示进行设备行为匹配，在28个公开MUD配置文件和80万条真实流量数据上验证了有效性。语义ACE匹配在观测初期和稀疏重叠场景下优于精确匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物联网设备识别方法依赖数据包或流记录等底层通信观测，其流量模式会随部署环境、软件版本和用户交互而变化，缺乏鲁棒性。作者旨在利用MUD配置文件中包含的设备行为策略（ACEs）进行更稳定的设备识别。

Method: 首先从28个公开MUD配置文件中提取1,023条ACE实例，构建ACE级别的紧凑行为文本语义表示，并分析其几何特性。然后设计语义ACE匹配方法，与精确ACE匹配在受控运行时变化（未知ACEs、主机名漂移、部分观测）下进行对比。最后在80万条真实流量轨迹上评估两种匹配方法在稳定重叠、稀疏重叠和早期观测等不同场景下的表现。

Result: ACE级别语义表示比完整配置文件的嵌入能更有效保留设备级别的行为区分性，且白化校准后仍保持有效。在运行时变化条件下，精确ACE匹配在重叠度高时表现良好，但重叠稀疏或消失时性能急剧下降；语义ACE匹配则能保持有用的识别证据。真实流量场景下，稳定重叠时精确匹配仍是最强信号，但语义匹配在早期观测阶段提供更强的识别证据，常能将正确设备保留在最高候选排名中，且在稀疏重叠条件下保持有效。

Conclusion: 语义ACE匹配是对现有精确匹配的有力补充，尤其适用于观测初期和稀疏重叠等实际部署中常见的困难场景，能提升物联网设备识别的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate identification of IoT devices is important for security management and policy enforcement. Existing approaches typically learn device signatures from packets or flow records. These methods operate on low-level communication observations whose traffic patterns may vary across deployments, software versions, and user interactions. This paper studies device identification using Manufacturer Usage Description (MUD) profiles. MUD profiles describe device behavior using Access Control Entries (ACEs), where each ACE represents a behavioral primitive consisting of protocol, endpoint, direction, and port semantics derived from device communication policy. Our contributions are threefold. First, using 28 publicly available MUD profiles containing 1,023 ACE instances, we construct ACE-level semantic representations from compact behavioral text and analyze their geometric properties. ACE-level representations preserve device-level behavioral distinctions more effectively than whole-profile embeddings and remain effective after whitening calibration. Second, we evaluate semantic ACE matching under controlled runtime variations, including unseen ACEs, drifted hostnames, and partial runtime observation. Exact ACE matching performs well when the overlap with the canonical MUD profile remains high, but degrades sharply when the overlap becomes sparse or disappears. In contrast, semantic ACE matching preserves useful identification evidence across these conditions. Third, we evaluate the same approach on real IoT traffic traces comprising more than 800,000 observed flows. Exact overlap remains the strongest signal when stable overlap exists, while semantic ACE matching provides stronger identification evidence during the early stages of observation, frequently retains the correct device among the highest-ranked candidates, and remains effective under sparse-overlap runtime traffic.

</details>


### [162] [A Privacy-Preserving Framework Using Remote Data Science for Inter-Institutional Student Retention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12845)
*John Fields,K M Sajjadul Islam,Ruchitha Thota,Victor Chen,Praveen Madiraju*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究基于PySyft平台构建了半气隙架构的远程数据科学框架，使三所大学的研究人员能在无需直接访问敏感学生数据的情况下协作预测学生保留率。实验验证了三种合成数据生成方法，并提出数据类型感知模板（Data-Type-Aware Templates），在严格遵循FERPA合规的同时，实现了跨机构一致的分类性能（Macro F1: 0.690-0.695），证明基于RDS的隐私保护机器学习在教育领域小规模跨机构协作中具有技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育机构间协作预测学生保留率时面临学生敏感数据的隐私保护挑战，传统联邦学习在小规模跨机构协作中存在局限性，亟需一种既能保护数据隐私又便于实施的替代方案。

Method: 采用PySyft平台构建远程数据科学（RDS）框架，设计半气隙架构（high-side和low-side服务器）；基于一所小型私立大学的历史数据（N=720），评估三种合成数据生成方法，并提出Data-Type-Aware Templates新型合成数据方法。

Result: 跨机构分类性能一致（Macro F1: 0.690-0.695），严格遵守FERPA合规要求；提出的数据类型感知模板方法在隐私保护与数据分布保真度之间取得了优先保障隐私的平衡。

Conclusion: 基于RDS的隐私保护机器学习在教育领域技术上可行，可作为联邦学习在小规模跨机构协作场景中的实用替代方案。

Abstract: This study explores privacy-preserving machine learning (PPML) techniques using the PySyft platform to enable collaborative prediction of student retention between institutions. We developed a remote data science (RDS) framework with a semi-air-gapped architecture consisting of high-side and low-side servers, allowing researchers from three universities to build predictive models on sensitive student data without direct data access. Using historical data from a small private university (N=720), we evaluated three synthetic data generation approaches and validated the framework through inter-institutional collaboration. The results demonstrate consistent classification performance across institutions (Macro F1: 0.690--0.695) while maintaining strict Family Educational Rights and Privacy Act (FERPA) compliance. We also propose Data-Type-Aware Templates, a novel synthetic data method that prioritizes privacy over distributional fidelity. Our findings confirm that RDS-based PPML is technically feasible for educational settings and offers a practical alternative to federated learning for small-scale inter-institutional collaborations. The code is available at https://github.com/jtfields/NAIRR240195-Privacy-Preserving-Machine-Learning.

</details>


### [163] [LNTest: A Testbed for Evaluating Bitcoin Lightning Network-Based Botnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12887)
*Thomas Bakaysa,Ahmet Kurt,Abdul-Salem Beibitkhan,Jesus Maria Romo Diaz de Leon,Tag Kalat,Joshua Kramer,Estela Rodriguez,Abraham Watkins,Abdullah Aydeger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了 LNTest——首个可复用的、基于 Lightning Network 的僵尸网络测试平台，通过在 Docker 容器化 Core Lightning 节点和 Bitcoin Core regtest 链上运行，支持三种覆盖拓扑模式。实验发现：(1) D-LNBot 的自主形成协议实际生成的是由桥接节点连接的聚类链，而非均匀链；(2) 指令传播复杂度为 Θ(n) 而非此前声称的 O(m log n)；(3) 不同覆盖拓扑对清除策略的鲁棒性截然不同，其中自主形成的聚类链在定向移除和随机失效下均最为脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Lightning Network 的僵尸网络设计方案（LNBot、D-LNBot）仅通过仿真进行了概念验证，缺乏对真实世界拓扑形成、传播复杂度和抗清除能力的实证研究，亟需一个可复现的实验平台来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建 LNTest 测试平台，基于 Docker 容器化的 Core Lightning 节点与共享的 Bitcoin Core regtest 链，支持确定性链、自主节点发现和用户提供图三种覆盖拓扑模式，以模拟不同僵尸网络结构并开展控制实验。

Result: (1) D-LNBot 自主形成协议产生聚类链，桥接节点移除即可碎片化网络；(2) 指令传播复杂度为线性 Θ(n)，与邻居连接数无关；(3) 均匀度链抗定向移除但怕随机失效，无标度拓扑相反，自主聚类链在两种攻击下均脆弱。

Conclusion: LNTest 作为开源可复现测试平台，能够系统评估 LN 僵尸网络的拓扑性质、传播效率与防御策略效果，为后续研究提供基础工具与关键实证发现。

Abstract: Bitcoin's Lightning Network (LN) can be exploited as a covert, low-cost command-and-control (C&C) channel for botnets, as demonstrated by the LNBot and D-LNBot designs. However, both remain proof-of-concept prototypes evaluated only through simulation, leaving key questions about real-world topology formation, propagation complexity, and resilience to takedowns unanswered. We present LNTest, the first reusable testbed for LN-based botnets, built from Core Lightning nodes containerized with Docker over a shared Bitcoin Core regtest chain. LNTest supports three overlay topology modes (a deterministic chain, autonomous peer discovery, and user-supplied graphs), enabling controlled experiments across different botnet structures. Using LNTest, we report three main findings. First, D-LNBot's autonomous formation protocol does not produce the uniform chain from its design; instead, it creates a clustered chain in which cliques are linked by bridge nodes whose removal fragments the network. Second, command propagation scales linearly with botnet size ($Θ(n)$), not the $O(m \log n)$ previously claimed, and gains nothing from higher neighbor connectivity. Third, the overlay topology determines the effectiveness of takedown strategies: uniform-degree chains resist targeted removal but fragment under random failure, scale-free topologies show the opposite pattern, and the autonomous clustered chain is fragile under both, making it the most vulnerable of the three. LNTest is released as open source, with a script that reproduces all our experiments, to support reproducible research on LN-based botnet defenses.

</details>


### [164] [MAStrike: Shapley-Guided Collusive Red-Teaming on Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12918)
*Chejian Xu,Zhaorun Chen,Jingyang Zhang,Freddy Lecue,Avni Kothari,Sarah Tan,Wenbo Guo,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MAStrike是一个针对层级式多智能体系统的闭环红队测试框架，通过Shapley值分析识别关键脆弱智能体并生成协同对抗攻击，实验表明其显著优于启发式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级式多智能体系统在高风险场景中快速部署，但其安全防御分布在不同角色的智能体上，攻击面显著扩大，现有红队测试方法依赖启发式选择和孤立消息扰动，无法回答哪些智能体对系统安全最负责以及被攻破的智能体如何协同绕过防御等关键问题。

Method: 提出首个面向MAS的智能体级Shapley值分析量化每个智能体对系统鲁棒性的边际贡献；基于此归因识别脆弱智能体联盟并生成协同的、角色感知的对抗操作；通过结构化因果诊断迭代优化攻击，将失败案例归因于阻碍攻击的未被攻破的智能体；构建了涵盖多种层级拓扑和领域的综合基准环境。

Result: 在基于多种前沿模型构建的MAS上的大量实验表明，MAStrike显著优于启发式基线。分析揭示了非平凡的Shapley值分布和智能体间高阶交互结构，发现了被先前方法忽略的关键脆弱性和协调模式。

Conclusion: MAStrike通过闭环框架有效解决了层级MAS中的协同红队测试问题，Shapley值分析能够识别系统中关键脆弱节点和智能体间的协同攻击模式，为MAS安全性评估提供了新方法。

Abstract: Hierarchical multi-agent systems (MAS) are rapidly being deployed in high-stakes workflows across domains such as finance and software engineering. In these systems, safety and security are inherently distributed across role-specialized agents, significantly expanding the attack surface, particularly under coordinated adversarial behaviors such as privilege escalation and cross-agent collusion. Existing red-teaming approaches for MAS remain limited: they rely on heuristic selection of target agents and perturb isolated message streams, leaving critical questions unanswered as which agents are most responsible for system safety, and how compromised agents can coordinate to bypass defenses. We propose MAStrike, a closed-loop framework for collusive red-teaming in hierarchical MAS. We propose the first agent-level Shapley value analysis for MAS, quantifying each agent's marginal contribution to system robustness under task-specific distributions. GGuided by this attribution, MAStrike identifies vulnerable agent coalitions and generates coordinated, role-aware adversarial manipulations. These attacks are iteratively refined through structured causal diagnosis, attributing failure cases to uncompromised agents that block adversarial attempts. We further build a comprehensive MAS red-teaming benchmark and controllable environments spanning diverse hierarchical topologies and domains, including finance, software engineering, and CRM. Extensive experiments across MAS built on multiple frontier models show that MAStrike substantially outperforms heuristic baselines. Our analysis further uncovers non-trivial Shapley value distributions and higher-order interaction structures among agents, revealing critical vulnerabilities and coordination patterns that are overlooked by prior single-agent or template-based methods.

</details>


### [165] [ViPER: Vision-based Packing-Aware Encoder for Robust Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12949)
*Fatima Qaiser,Bisma Tahir,Muhammad Abid Mughal,Nauman Shamim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ViPER提出了一种基于视觉的打包感知编码器，通过LoRA适配的ViT-B/14骨干网络和双头架构，联合学习恶意软件检测和打包检测，并利用打包感知门控机制在不同打包状态下采用不同决策边界，在20万Windows PE字节图图像上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可视化的恶意软件检测方法在遇到可执行文件打包时会产生高熵图像，掩盖结构化模式，导致模型失效。由于打包在良性软件中也普遍存在，仅凭打包状态无法可靠判断恶意性，而现有方法未能在统一监督框架下解决这一挑战。

Method: ViPER采用LoRA适配的ViT-B/14骨干网络，设计双头架构同时学习恶意软件分类和打包检测；引入打包感知门控机制，根据推断的打包状态调整恶意软件预测的决策边界；在训练中使用频率加权损失和基于联合类别-打包分层的分层采样来处理打包标签分布不平衡问题。

Result: 在20万Windows PE字节图图像上，ViPER的平衡准确率为0.8521，ROC-AUC为0.9260，AUPR为0.9279，在所有主要指标上均优于代表性基线方法；打包检测AUC达到0.9949。

Conclusion: ViPER通过显式建模打包状态并将其纳入恶意软件检测决策过程，有效解决了打包带来的性能退化问题，为视觉化恶意软件检测提供了一种鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Visualization-based malware detection maps raw binary bytes to grayscale images and applies learned visual classifiers, providing an evasion-resistant and disassembly-free alternative to conventional analysis pipelines. However, executable packing remains a critical failure mode: packed binaries produce high-entropy images that obscure the structural patterns these models rely on. Because packing is also prevalent in benign software (e.g., for compression or copy protection), packing state alone is not a reliable indicator of maliciousness, and existing approaches do not address this challenge within a unified supervised framework. We present ViPER, a Vision-based Packing-Aware Encoder for Robust malware detection. ViPER builds on a LoRA-adapted ViT-B/14 backbone with a dual-head architecture that jointly learns malware classification and packing detection. A packing-aware gating mechanism conditions malware predictions on the inferred packing state, enabling distinct decision boundaries for packed and unpacked inputs. To address packing label skew during training, we employ frequency-weighted losses with stratified sampling over joint class-packing strata. Evaluated on 200,000 Windows PE byteplot images, ViPER achieves a balanced accuracy of 0.8521, ROC-AUC of 0.9260, and AUPR of 0.9279, outperforming representative state-of-the-art baselines across all primary metrics, while attaining a packing detection AUC of 0.9949.

</details>


### [166] [SoK: The Constant Time Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13000)
*Billy Bob Brumley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化了常量时间编程模型及其演变，揭示了模型保护范围与规范假设之间的差距，并提出了一种针对密码学原语边界外的时序攻击的进攻性方法论。利用该方法发现了与私钥加载相关的规范级漏洞，并在OpenSSL和BoringSSL中确认了信息泄露，且BoringSSL尽管威胁模型更严格，其单次观测信号强度却比OpenSSL高出数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "常量时间"在不同学术界和工业界定义不一，且现有保护模型与密码学规范的实际假设之间存在未被充分认识的差距，导致攻击面被忽视。

Method: 系统化梳理常量时间模型及其演进，识别模型与规范之间的鸿沟，提炼出针对密码学原语边界之外的时序漏洞发现方法论。

Result: 发现了一个与私钥加载相关的规范级时序漏洞，并在OpenSSL和BoringSSL中确认了实际泄露；BoringSSL虽威胁模型更强，但其单次观测信号强度比OpenSSL高出多个数量级。

Conclusion: 现有常量时间模型存在系统性盲区，攻击者可以利用模型与规范之间的差距，针对密码学原语边界外的实现环节发起有效攻击，并且更严格的威胁模型可能引入更强的侧信道信号。

Abstract: Constant time programming patterns is the primary defense against timing attacks on cryptographic implementations, yet what "constant time" means varies across academia and industry. This work systematizes constant time models and their evolution, identifies a recurring gap between what models protect and what specifications assume, and distills an offensive methodology for discovering timing vulnerabilities that originate outside the cryptographic primitive boundary. Applying this methodology, we locate a specification-level vulnerability related to private key loading, and confirm the leak in both OpenSSL and BoringSSL. Counterintuitively, BoringSSL's per-observation signal is several orders of magnitude stronger than OpenSSL's, despite an explicitly stricter threat model.

</details>


### [167] [DIG: Oracle-Guided Directed Input Generation for One-Day Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13037)
*Andrew Bao,Haochen Zeng,Peng Chen,Stephen McCamant,Pen-Chung Yew*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DIG是一种利用大语言模型分析补丁、合成oracle来显式表达漏洞触发条件，并结合oracle引导的生成器演化和分支距离反馈指导定向模糊测试，从而高效生成one-day漏洞PoC输入的方法。在138个真实CVE上，DIG触发了80个漏洞，相比最优基线提升40%，并独家触发了9个现有技术无法触发的漏洞，同时发现了6个零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: One-day漏洞因补丁延迟或不完全部署带来重大风险，需要生成PoC输入以评估实际影响。现有定向模糊测试方法依赖目标距离反馈和随机变异，未能有效识别和求解必要约束；智能体方法虽具潜力，但长程推理中的目标漂移限制了其有效性。DIG旨在解决这些不足。

Method: DIG利用one-day漏洞的关键特性：补丁通常揭示了触发漏洞的必要前置条件。DIG使用LLM分析补丁并合成oracle，显式表达这些条件。在高层，DIG执行oracle引导的生成器演化，由智能体推断并求解约束以满足oracle；在低层，DIG将oracle插桩到目标程序中，利用分支距离反馈指导定向模糊测试中的随机变异。

Result: 在138个真实CVE上，DIG触发了80个漏洞，超越最优基线40%（57 vs 80）。DIG独家触发9个现有技术无法触发的漏洞。相比其他工具平均，DIG在92.9%的案例中更快触发漏洞，48.8%的案例实现超过100倍加速，最大加速达3,664倍。此外，DIG还发现了广泛部署库中的6个未知零日漏洞。

Conclusion: DIG通过补丁驱动的oracle合成与双层次引导策略，显著提升了one-day漏洞PoC生成的效率和覆盖率，并具备零日漏洞发现能力，验证了其方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: One-day vulnerabilities pose significant risks due to delayed or incomplete patch adoption. Generating proof-of-concept (PoC) inputs is therefore essential for assessing real-world impact. The key challenge is identifying necessary constraints for triggering the vulnerability and solving them effectively. Existing directed fuzzing approaches prioritize inputs toward target locations, but neither explicitly identify necessary constraints nor solve them effectively, relying instead on target-distance feedback and random mutation. Agentic approaches show strong potential through code reasoning and structured input generation, but goal drift in long-horizon reasoning limits their effectiveness.
  DIG addresses this challenge by exploiting a key property of one-day vulnerabilities: patches often reveal necessary preconditions for triggering. DIG uses an LLM to analyze the patch and synthesize an oracle making these conditions explicit. The oracle supports effective PoC generation at two levels. At the high level, DIG performs oracle-guided generator evolution, where an agent infers and solves constraints to satisfy the oracle. At the low level, DIG instruments the oracle into the target program and uses branch-distance feedback to guide random mutation in directed fuzzing. Evaluation shows DIG outperforms 2 state-of-the-art agents and 10 fuzzers across 138 real-world CVEs. DIG triggers 80 vulnerabilities, surpassing prior results and outperforming the best baseline by 40% (57 vs. 80 CVEs). Notably, DIG exclusively triggers 9 vulnerabilities no existing technique can trigger. Compared to the average of other tools, DIG triggers vulnerabilities faster in 92.9% of cases, achieving over 100x speedup in 48.8% of cases, with a maximum speedup of 3,664x. Beyond one-day PoC generation, DIG uncovers 6 previously unknown vulnerabilities in widely deployed libraries, enabling zero-day discovery.

</details>


### [168] [The Emergence of Autonomous Penetration Capabilities in Large Language Model-Powered AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13079)
*Jiaqi Luo,Jiarun Dai,Zhile Chen,Jia Xu,Weibing Wang,Yawen Duan,Brian Tse,Geng Hong,Xudong Pan,Yuan Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个新的自主渗透评估框架，包含两级目标服务器（共300台）和通用代理架构，评估了19个开源和商业LLM，发现自主渗透成功率在10.7%到69.3%之间，且该能力随模型整体能力提升而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自主渗透能力评估存在方法不透明、场景过于简化、模型被赋予过多先验知识等问题，无法准确反映AI系统在真实高影响网络攻击场景中的自主渗透能力。

Method: 构建包含目标服务器和代理脚手架两部分的评估框架：目标服务器端设计Tier-1（一个安全服务）和Tier-2（三个安全服务）两级环境共300台服务器；代理脚手架采用通用代理架构配备通用网络安全工具，无目标特定先验知识；评估19个开源和商业LLM。

Result: 当前模型自主渗透成功率在10.7%到69.3%之间；自主渗透能力随模型整体能力提升而持续改善。

Conclusion: 现有AI系统的自主渗透能力尚有限（最高不到70%），但该能力与模型总体能力呈正相关趋势，需持续关注其对高影响网络攻击红线的潜在威胁。

Abstract: Nowadays, the autonomous execution of cyberattacks capable of causing substantial real-world harm is widely regarded as one of the critical red lines that frontier AI systems must not cross. Within this broader red-line scenario, autonomous penetration represents a core enabling capability and subtask: the ability of LLM-powered AI systems to independently conduct adversarial operations against a target server without human intervention, identify and exploit vulnerabilities, and obtain unauthorized access or control. A growing body of work has sought to assess the autonomous penetration capabilities of AI systems. However, existing evaluations often employ opaque methodologies, rely on unrealistic or overly simplified penetration-testing scenarios, or provide LLMs with excessive prior knowledge and task-specific guidance, and cannot accurately capture the extent to which modern AI systems can autonomously perform this core capability within broader high-impact cyberattack scenarios.
  To address these limitations, we construct a new autonomous penetration evaluation framework consisting of two components: target servers and agent scaffolding. Specifically, on the target-server side, we design two levels of target environments based on the number of secure services without known vulnerabilities deployed alongside a vulnerable service: Tier~1 (one secure service) and Tier~2 (three secure services), resulting in a total of 300 target servers. Meanwhile, the agent scaffolding adopts a general-purpose agent architecture equipped with a set of general-purpose cybersecurity tools, without any target-specific prior knowledge. We evaluate 19 open-weight and proprietary LLMs, and find that current models achieve penetration success rates ranging from 10.7% to 69.3%. Moreover, we observe that autonomous penetration capability continues to improve alongside advances in overall model capability.

</details>


### [169] [The Invisible Ink of the Android Malware World: A Longitudinal Study on the Usage of Covert Communication Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13107)
*Zeya Umayya,Manan Aggarwal,Manan Chugh,Mann Nariya,Yogesh Kaushik,Sambuddho Chakravarty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对Android恶意软件生态系统中隐蔽信道（CC）的使用进行了纵向研究，基于2009年至2025年间的350万恶意APK样本，发现CC使用率从2012年的0.30%激增至2025年的50%，涉及511个恶意软件家族，并识别出85个国家的19,308个唯一IP地址。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管代理、VPN和Tor等工具既用于隐私保护也可能被恶意软件利用，但缺乏针对恶意软件如何利用隐蔽信道规避检测的纵向研究。

Method: 开发了一个结合静态与动态分析的多阶段流水线，对350万Android恶意样本（2009-2025年）进行系统和网络级特征分析，通过静态验证规则筛选出使用CC的APK，并在动态分析中验证IP地址的CC行为。

Result: 发现288K APK使用CC，涉及511个恶意软件家族；CC使用率从0.30%（2012年）增长至50%（2025年）；动态分析识别出85个国家的19,308个IP，其中17个国家的59个IP明确验证存在CC；发现CC使用行为随时间演变，如部分恶意软件使用多个CC或周期性切换（某个家族在2019-2025年间切换40次）。

Conclusion: Android恶意软件中隐蔽信道的使用呈指数增长且行为日趋复杂，表明CC已成为恶意软件规避检测的重要手段，亟需加强相关检测与防御措施。

Abstract: Proxies, VPNs and Tor have long helped the privacy community and users in censored regions to fight censorship. However, the same tools can be maliciously exploited by malware and botnets to conceal their communication to external command and control servers. Despite being a critical concern fueled by the proliferation of malware based attacks, no longitudinal studies have analyzed how malware applications use covert channels (CC) to evade detection.
  We fill this gap by performing the first study of the usage of covert channels in the Android malware ecosystem. To that end, we develop a multistage pipeline that combines static and dynamic analysis to investigate both system and network-level features. We applied this pipeline on a corpus of 3.5M Android malware spanning 2009 to July 2025. Our carefully crafted static validation rules uncovered 288K APKs that used CCs spanning 511 malware families and CC usage growing exponentially from 0.30\% (2012) to 50\% (2025). Overall, in dynamic analysis, we identified 19,308 unique IP addresses being contacted in 85 countries, out of which we were able to explicitly validate the presence of CCs for 59 IP addresses across 17 countries. Further, we performed a longitudinal dataset study spanning over 16 years for CC based malware and found that CC usage has evolved, \textit{e.g.,} some malware adopted by using more than one CCs; others switched between them periodically (one family switched CC usage 40 times from 2019 to 2025).

</details>


### [170] [Who Pays the Price? Stakeholder-Centric Prompt Injection Benchmarking for Real-world Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13385)
*Zihao Wang,Yiming Li,Yutong Wu,Zheyu Liu,Kangjie Chen,Fok Kar Wai,Pin-Yu Chen,Vrizlynn L. L. Thing,Bo Li,Dacheng Tao,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以利益相关者为中心的基准测试 StakeBench，用于系统性地分类和归因 Web 代理系统中的提示注入攻击危害，揭示了现有攻击中心视角的不足，并发现当前 LLM 代理无法可靠抵御任何攻击目标，且失败模式呈现高度异质性（如隐蔽寄生、错位干扰、复合失败）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准采用攻击中心视角，仅关注注入的技术可行性，忽略了危害在利益相关者间的差异化分布——同一攻击对不同受害者（用户、卖家、平台）的后果不对称，且攻击效果因目标不同而异。

Method: 构建 StakeBench 基准：区分受影响的实体（用户、卖家、平台），将攻击分解为具体目标，并用结果级和过程级指标补充评估每个案例。

Result: 当前代理无法可靠抵御任何单一攻击目标；失败模式涵盖隐蔽寄生（攻击成功但用户任务未中断）、错位干扰（任务中断但攻击未成功）和复合失败（攻击目标与任务完整性同时被破坏）等多种模式。

Conclusion: 传统评估方法会遗漏上述重要失败模式，凸显了在真实部署中对 LLM 代理进行利益相关者感知评估的必要性。

Abstract: Web agents driven by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world environments, where they operate over untrusted web content and execute actions with direct consequences. This makes them vulnerable to prompt-injection attacks, in which seemingly benign content embeds adversarial instructions that manipulate agent behaviour. Existing security benchmarks adopt an \textit{attack-centric} perspective, focusing on the technical feasibility of injections while overlooking the nuanced distribution of resulting harms. In practice, however, prompt-injection risk is victim-dependent: a single exploit can produce asymmetric consequences for different stakeholders, and the same attack pattern may exhibit substantially different effectiveness depending on whom it targets. To capture these properties, we introduce \textbf{\sysname}, a \textit{stakeholder-centric} benchmark to systematically categorize and attribute harm in real-world web agent systems. It distinguishes between affected entities (e.g., user, seller, platform), decomposes the attacks into concrete objectives, and evaluates each case with complementary outcome- and process-level metrics. Our results reveal substantial and heterogeneous vulnerabilities: not a single attack objective is reliably resisted by current agents, and failures distribute across qualitatively distinct modes ranging from \emph{stealthy parasitism} (attack succeeds without disrupting the user's delegated task) to \emph{misaligned disruption} (task disrupted without attack success) and \emph{compounded failure} (both adversarial objective and task integrity simultaneously violated). These patterns are missed by conventional evaluation, highlighting the need for stakeholder-aware assessment of LLM-based agents in real-world deployments. Benchmark is available at https://github.com/StakeBench/SBC.

</details>


### [171] [An Assessment Framework for Application-Level Cryptographic Agility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13425)
*Navaneeth Rameshan,Gregoire Messmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对后量子密码迁移中API缺乏敏捷性的问题，提出了一个七维组件化评估框架，用于系统性地衡量应用程序级密码敏捷性。通过对六个代表性API的评估，发现了三个普遍且独立的能力缺口：缺乏意图驱动的密钥创建、策略驱动的算法选择（区别于访问控制）以及现有密钥的算法转换操作。这些缺口足以阻碍敏捷迁移，解释了为何后量子密码迁移在API发展数十年后仍是软件工程难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码迁移需要完全替换所有软件中的密码算法，但现有密码API在设计时未考虑敏捷性，且缺乏系统性评估密码敏捷性的方法。此外，"密码敏捷性"这一术语本身涵盖多种独立能力（替换算法、按策略选择、替换实现），这种缺乏结构化分解的现状限制了系统评估和敏捷API的开发。

Method: 提出了一个组件化评估框架，从七个正交维度刻画应用程序级密码敏捷性：三个耦合维度（衡量应用代码对算法和提供者的了解程度）、一个横切解耦机制、一个治理权威维度，以及两个敏捷性使能维度（衡量实际迁移能力）。该框架是非线性的，能够捕获非层次化的敏捷性画像。使用该框架评估了六个代表性API：PKCS#11、OpenSSL 3.0、JCA、Google Tink、AWS KMS和HashiCorp Vault Transit。

Result: 评估揭示了三个普遍存在且相互独立的能力缺口：1）没有系统支持意图驱动的密钥创建；2）没有任何系统提供策略驱动的算法选择（区别于访问控制）；3）没有任何系统提供专门的一等操作用于对现有密钥进行算法转换。这些缺口单独存在即足以阻碍敏捷迁移。

Conclusion: 当前API存在三个根本性能力缺失，这些缺口是后量子密码迁移仍是软件工程难题的根本原因。该框架为评估和指导密码敏捷API的开发提供了系统化的理论基础。

Abstract: The impending post-quantum transition to new cryptography will require complete replacement of algorithms within all software. The cryptographic APIs used today make this transition challenging because they were not designed with agility as a concern. There is no method for systematically assessing cryptographic agility as an overall ability. In addition to this, the term itself refers to multiple independent capabilities. Specifically, it includes replacing algorithms, selecting by policy, and substituting implementations. This lack of structured decomposition limits both the evaluation of systems and the development of cryptographically agile APIs.
  We introduce a component-based assessment framework that characterizes application-level cryptographic agility along seven orthogonal dimensions: three coupling dimensions that measure what the application code knows about algorithms and providers, a cross-cutting decoupling mechanism, a governance authority dimension, and two agility enablers that measure actual migration capability. The framework is non-linear and captures non-hierarchical profiles: a system may achieve high operation decoupling yet low creation decoupling, or strong versioning without externalized configuration.
  We evaluate six representative APIs (PKCS#11, OpenSSL~3.0, JCA, Google Tink, AWS KMS, and HashiCorp Vault Transit) against the framework, revealing three pervasive and independent gaps: no system supports intent-based key creation, none provides policy-driven algorithm selection (as distinct from access control), and none offers dedicated/first-class operations for algorithm transformation of existing keys. These gaps are individually sufficient to prevent agile migration, explaining why the post-quantum transition remains a software engineering problem despite decades of API progress.

</details>


### [172] [Intent-Based Cryptographic API Design for Cryptographic Agility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13445)
*Navaneeth Rameshan,Gregoire Messmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套密码学敏捷API的设计原则，通过意图词汇、抽象策略API和密钥演化操作，使密码学算法更新成为无需重写应用代码的运营流程，解决向抗量子密码迁移中的软件工程难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在向后量子密码学迁移时面临巨大挑战：现有密码学API围绕特定算法设计，缺乏策略驱动的算法选择支持，无法直接迁移现有密钥，这使得算法过渡成为一个软件工程问题。

Method: 从五大基本架构特性（抽象性、稳定性、时间灵活性、分离性、可扩展性）推导出设计原则；通过Protocol Buffers API设计模式示例实现；提出基于作用域的意图词汇实现密钥创建与算法身份解耦；设计抽象策略API支持密钥演化操作（轮转、转换、迁移）。

Result: 实现了密钥创建与算法身份的完全解耦，支持算法在作用域内的透明替换；通过抽象策略API实现密码学治理；密钥支持演化操作，可在不同算法和提供商之间迁移的同时追踪原始密钥身份和演化历史。

Conclusion: 采用该密码学敏捷API方法，更新密码学算法成为纯粹的运营流程，无需重写应用程序代码，有效解决了向抗量子密码迁移中的软件工程挑战。

Abstract: As organizations move toward post-quantum cryptography, they face the major challenge of updating cryptographic algorithms across large, complex software portfolios. However, most cryptographic APIs in use today were designed around specific algorithms. These APIs expect explicit use of specific algorithms, provide little or no support for policy-based algorithm selection, and offer no straightforward way to migrate existing keys to newer algorithms. This makes the transition to post-quantum cryptography challenging. The companion assessment framework identifies the barriers to cryptographic agility and explains why algorithm transition is largely a software engineering problem.
  To address the limitations of current cryptographic APIs, we identify the principles necessary to design a cryptographically agile API. The design principles are derived from five fundamental architectural characteristics (Abstraction, Stability, Temporal Flexibility, Separation, and Extensibility). We also show how the design principles can be implemented using several examples of Protocol Buffers API design patterns. In particular, we present an intent vocabulary that is based on scopes which allows for decoupling key creation from algorithm identities. It also supports transparent substitutions of algorithms in the applicable scope. Cryptographic governance is enabled by an abstract policy API that does not prescribe the policy format. Keys are represented by stable identifiers and support key evolution operations (rotation, transformation, migration), facilitating migration between algorithms and providers while tracking both the original key identity and its evolution history. With this approach, updating cryptography becomes an operational process without the need to rewrite application code.

</details>


### [173] [Differentially Private Hierarchical Heavy Hitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13563)
*Ari Biswas,Graham Cormode,Yaron Kanza,Divesh Srivastava,Zhengyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了在差分隐私保护下，于流式和非流式场景中发布层次化重击者（HHH）的问题。非流式场景中，前缀残差计数的相对误差与层次高度和重击者数量无关；流式场景中，尽管近似函数具有高全局敏感性，但频率估计的绝对误差与可用空间无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管层次化重击者（HHH）问题在数据流中被广泛研究，但数据隐私保护场景下的HHH发布问题此前尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究差分隐私约束下HHH的发布方法。

Method: 分别在非流式（离线）和流式（在线）设置下分析HHH的差分隐私发布。非流式场景中，利用残差计数前缀的相对误差性质；流式场景中，分析近似函数的高全局敏感性并证明绝对误差的独立性。

Result: 非流式场景：任意前缀的残差计数相对误差与层次高度和重击者数量无关。流式场景：尽管近似函数的全局敏感性与可用空间线性相关，但频率估计的绝对误差与可用空间无关。

Conclusion: 本文为差分隐私下的HHH发布问题提供了理论保证，分别在非流式和流式场景中给出了误差独立性的关键结论，为后续隐私保护的流数据处理奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: The task of finding _Hierarchical_ Heavy Hitters (HHH) was introduced by Cormode et al. [VLDB 2003] as a generalisation of the heavy hitter problem. While finding HHH in data streams has been studied extensively, the question of releasing HHH when the underlying data is private remains unexplored. In this paper, we study differentially private HHH release in both the streaming and non-streaming setting. In the non-streaming setting, we show the surprising result that the relative error in estimating the residual count for any prefix is independent of the height of the hierarchy and the number of heavy hitters in the stream. Meanwhile, in the streaming setting, although the exact version of HHH has low global sensitivity (as counting queries are 1-sensitive), the approximation functions due to streaming have high global sensitivity, linear in the available space. Despite this obstacle, we show that the absolute error for estimating frequencies in the steaming setting is independent of the available space.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond the IT Checklist: Engineering a Reasonable Standard of Care for Cyber Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13612)
*Matthew E. Jablonski,Linton Wells,Kathryn B. Laskey,F. Brett Berlin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了美国网络安全政策（2000-2025年间292份关键基础设施政策文件）如何将"合理注意"操作化，发现义务集中在"预期"阶段且强调行政合规，而"承受"和"恢复"阶段过度依赖与物理危害不适配的IT控制目录。研究识别出三项脱节：标准校准不当、恢复被定义为通知而非工程化导航、行业间适应性要求不均衡。提出以危害特定可追溯性、结构化保证案例和网络弹性工程为核心的现代化注意标准，并建议联邦政策配合定向激励使弹性架构成为可行商业决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前美国网络政策以安全为中心，将控制文档和事件报告作为安全性的代理指标，这种方法在数字故障可能产生物理伤害的网络-物理系统中存在根本性不足。作者试图揭示这种合规导向路径与真正物理安全需求之间的系统性脱节。

Method: 构建并编码了包含292份关键基础设施政策文档（2000-2025年）的语料库，沿NIST SP 800-160 Vol.2弹性生命周期（预期、承受、恢复等阶段）分析"合理注意"的操作化方式，通过映射揭示义务分布和引用关系。

Result: 义务集中在"预期"阶段且强调行政合规；"承受"和"恢复"阶段大量依赖对IT控制目录的委托引用，与物理危害不匹配。识别出三大脱节：校准不当的委托标准、将恢复定义为通知而非工程化导航、行业间适应性要求不均衡。

Conclusion: 需要建立以危害特定可追溯性、结构化保证案例和网络弹性工程为基础的现代化注意标准，联邦政策应配合定向激励，使关键基础设施的弹性架构成为可行的商业决策。

Abstract: Current U.S. cyber policy, centered on security, often treats documentation of controls and incident reports as a proxy for safety in the built environment. This paper argues that such an approach is inadequate for cyber-physical systems, where digital failures can produce kinetic harm. We construct and code a corpus of critical infrastructure policy documents (N=292, 2000-2025) to examine how "reasonable care" is operationalized across the NIST SP 800-160 Vol.~2 resilience lifecycle. The resulting maps show that obligations are concentrated in the Anticipate phase and emphasize administrative compliance, while Withstand and Recover phases rely heavily on delegated references to IT-focused control catalogs that are poorly aligned with physics-based hazards. We identify three major disconnects: miscalibrated delegated standards, recovery defined as notification rather than engineered navigation, and uneven adaptation requirements across sectors. We then propose a modernized standard of care anchored in hazard-specific traceability, structured assurance cases, and cyber resiliency engineering. Finally, we recommend that federal policy pair these engineering obligations with targeted incentives so that resilient architectures for critical infrastructure become a viable business decision rather than an unfunded expectation.

</details>
